2a1(khri pa): @##//bcom ldan 'das shes

3a1(khri pa): @##//dpa' sems dpa' chen pos shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la ji ltar spyad par bgyi lags/bac+dos kyis bkA 
3a2(khri pa): stsal pa/rab 'byor 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa la spyod pa ni gang gi gzuT 
3a3(khri pa): la mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu pyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed 
3a4(khri pa): rnams dang /rnam par shes pa la mi spyod na de ni shes rang kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du sbyar te rnam 
3a5(khri pa): pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar la mi spyod na de ni shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa spyod pa yin no//gzugs rtag ces bya ba 'am/
3b1(khri pa): mi rtag ces bya bar mi spyod na/de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor 
3b2(khri pa): ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa rtag ces bya ba 'am/mi 
3b3(khri pa): rtag ces bya bar mi spyod pa de ni shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa la spyod pa yin//
3b4(khri pa): gzugs bde zhes bya ba 'am/sdug bsngal zhes bya bar mi spyod na/de'i shes

4a1(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//gzugs 
4a2(khri pa): bdag ces bya ba 'am/bdag med ces bya bar mi spyod na/de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na ba 
4a3(khri pa): la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed 
4a4(khri pa): rnams dang /rnam pa ra shes pa bdag ces bya ba 'am/bag med ces bya bar mi 
4a5(khri pa): spyod na/de ni shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyo pa yin no//gzugs stong 
4a6(khri pa): zhes bya ba 'am/mi stong zhes bya bar mi spyod na/des ni shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 
4a7(khri pa): la spyod pa yin no/de bzhin na du tshor ba dang /'du zhes dang /
4b1(khri pa): 'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa stong zhes bya ba 'am/mi stong zhes bya bar mi spyod na/de ni shes rab kyi 
4b2(khri pa): pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//gzugs mtshan ma yod ces bya ba 'am/mtshan ma med ces bya bar mi spyod na /
4b3(khri pa): med ces bya bar mi spyod pa yin no//de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod/pa yin no//de bzhin 
4b4(khri pa): du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /dang /rnam par 
4b5(khri pa): shes pa mtshan ma yod ces bya ba'am/mtshan ma med ces ba bar mi spyod na/

5a1(khri pa): @#//spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa smon pa yod ces bya ba 'am smon pa med ces bya bar mi spyod na de ni
5a2(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//gzugs zhi zhes bya ba 'am ma zhi zhes bya bar mi spyod na de na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhinadu tshor ba dang /'du
5a3(khri pa): shes dang /'du byed rnamasadang /rnam par shes pa zhi zhes bya 'am ma zhi zhes bya bar ma spyi da ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu yin pa la spyod pa yin no//gzugs dben zhes bya ba 'am mi dben zhes bya
5a4(khri pa): bar mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor bdang /'du shes dang /mdu byed rnams dang /rnams par shes pa dben zhes bya ba'am
5a5(khri pa): mi dben zhes bya bar mi spyod na da ni shes rab kyi pha roladu phyin pa la spyod pa yin/gzugs kun nas nyon mongs pa zhes bya ba 'am rnam par byang zhes bya bar mi spyod na ni shes
5a6(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pal spyod pa yin no//de nyid du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa kun nas nyon mongs pa stes bya ba 'am rnam par byung ba zhes bya bar
5a7(khri pa): mi spyod na de ni shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//gzugs skye zhes bya ba 'am mi skye zhes bya bar mi spyod na/de ni shes rab kyi pha rold du phyin pa la spyod pa yi no//de bzhin du tshor ba
5a8(khri pa): dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnamapar shes pa skye zhes bya ba 'am/mi skye zhes bya bar mi spyodan de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyodapa yin no//gzugs 'gag ces bya
5b1(khri pa): ba 'am/mi 'gag ces bya bar mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa 'gag ces bya ba 'am/
5b2(khri pa): mi 'gag ces bya bar mi sbyod na de ni shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa la spyod yin no//gzugs dngos po yod ces bya ba 'am dngos po med ces bya bar mi spyod na ni shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la spyod pa 
5b3(khri pa): yin no//de bzhin du tshorab dang /'du shes dang 'du byed rnams dang /rnal par shes pa dngos po yod ces bya ba'am dngos po med ces bya par mi spyod na de ni shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa spyod pa yin 
5b4(khri pa): no||gzugs gtsang zhes bya ba 'am mi gtsang zhes bya bar mi spyod de ni shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa la spyod pa yi no//de bzhin du tshor pa dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /
5b5(khri pa): rnam par shes pa gtsang zhes bya ba 'am mi btsang zhes bya bar mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yan no//de bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar 
5b6(khri pa): du rtag ces bya ba 'am mi rtag ces bya ba nas de bzhin du mig gi bar la gtsang zhes bya ba 'am migatsang zhes bya bar mi spyod pa ni shes rab kyi pha rol te phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/
5b7(khri pa): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod pa gzugs rtag ces bya ba 'am mi gcag ces bya bar yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ngo //de bzhin du bde zhes bya ba 'am/
5b8(khri pa): sdug bsngal zhes bya ba 'am/bdag ces bya ba 'am/bdag med ces bya ba 'am/stong zhes bya ba 'am/mi stong zhes bya ba 'am/mtshan ma yod ces bya ba 'am/mtshan ma med ces 
6a1(khri pa): @#//bya ba 'am/smon pa yod ces bya ba 'am/smon pa med ces bya ba 'am/zhi zhes bya ba 'am/ma zhi zhes bya ba 'am/dben zhes bya ba 'am/mi dben zhes bya ba 'am/
6a2(khri pa): kun nas nyon mongs zhes bya ba 'am/rnam par byang zhes bya ba 'am/skye zhes bya ba 'am/'gag ces bya ba 'am/dngos po yod ces bya ba 'am/dngos bo med ces bya ba 'am/gtsang zhes bya 
6a3(khri pa): ba 'am/mi gtsang zhes bya bar yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ngo//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa dang /de bzhin du rnam pa thams cad 
6a4(khri pa): mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du rtag ces bya ba 'am/mi rtag ces bya ba 'am/bde zhes bya ba 'am/sdug bsngal zhes bya ba 'am/bdag ces bya ba 'am/bdag med ces bya ba 'am/
6a5(khri pa): de bzhin du sbyar te gtsang zhes bya ba 'am/mi gtsang zhes bya'i bar du yang dag par rjes su mi 'thong ba'i phyir ro//rab 'byor gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
6a6(khri pa): chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa na/gzugs yongs su rdzogs pa zhes bya ba 'am/yongs su ma rdzogs zhes bya bar mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod pa yin 
6a7(khri pa): no||de bzhin du tshor ba dang 'du shes dang 'du byed rnams dang rnam par shes pa yongs su rdzogs shes bya ba 'am/yongs sum rdzogs shes bya bar mi spyod na de na shes rab kyi pha rol 
6a8(khri pa): tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar dag yongs su rdzogs zhes bya ba 'am yongs su ma rdzogs zhes bya par mi spyod na de ni shes ram kyi pha rol tu 
6b1(khri pa): phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa na/gzugs yang dag par rjes su mi mthong 
6b2(khri pa): mi dmigs la/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa yang dag par rjes su mi 'thong mi dmigs shing /de bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen 
6b3(khri pa): pa nyid kyi bar du yang dag par rjes su mi  mthong mi dmigs pa'i phyir ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ji tsam du bcom 
6b4(khri pa): ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo rnams kyi chags pa ma mchis pa de dag legs par bshad pa ngo mtshar lags so//bcom ldan 
6b5(khri pa): 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor de de bzhin no//ji skad smras pa de bzhin na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ma pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis byang chub 
6b6(khri pa): sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i ris kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bum mo rnams kyi chags pa med pa dag legs par bshad pa yin no//rab 'byor gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab 
6b7(khri pa): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa na/gzugs chags zhes bya ba 'am/ma chags zhes bya bar mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyad pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes 
6b8(khri pa): dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa chags zhes bya ba 'am ma chags zhes bya bar mi sbyod na de ni shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa la spyod yin no//de bzhin du mig dang /rna ba dang /sna dang /
7a1(khri pa): @#//lce dang /lus dang /yid chags zhes bya ba 'am/ma chags zhes bya bar mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pi yan no//de bzhin du gzugs dang /
7a2(khri pa): sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg dang /chos rnams chags zhes bya ba 'am//ma chags zhes bya bar mi sbyod na de ni shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa la spyod yin no//de bzhin du sbyin pa'i pha 
7a3(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa dang /tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dag/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang shes rab kyi pha 
7a4(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa chags zhes bya ba 'am ma chags zhes bya bar mi skyo da na de ni shes rab kyi pharol tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du sbyar te dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams chags zhes bya ba
7a5(khri pa): 'am|ma chags zhes bya bar mi spyad na de ni shes rab kyi pharol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i yan no//de bzhin du 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa chags zhes bya ba 'amam chags
7a6(khri pa): zhes bya bar mi spyod na de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod payin no//de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu chags zhes bya ba 'am mchags zhes bya bar mi spyod na deni shes rab kyi pha rol du
7a7(khri pa): phyin pa la spyod pa yin no//de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad chags zhes bya ba 'am mngags shes bya bar mi spyod na de na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod pa yin no/de 
7a8(khri pa): bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du chags zhes bya ba 'am ma chags zhes bya bar mi spyod de ni shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa la spyod pa yin no //rab 'byor byang chub sems
7b1(khri pa): dpa' sems dpa' chen po de ltar spyod na gzugs chags zhes bya bar yang dag par shes so//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa chags shes 
7b2(khri pa): bya bar yang dag par shes swo//de bzhin du sbyar te rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu chags zhes bya bar yang dang dag par shes so//de gzhan du lan gcig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /phyir mi 'ong ba'i 
7b3(khri pa): 'bras bu dang /dgra bcom pa nyid dang /rang byang chub dang /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi bar du chags shes bya bar yang dag par zhes so//de nas bcom ldan 'das la 
7b4(khri pa): tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ji tsam du chos zab mo 'di ma bshad kyang 'grib pa mchis la/bshad kyang 'phel ba ma mchis 
7b5(khri pa): pa ni ngo mtshar lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor de de bzhin no//ji skad 
7b6(khri pa): smras pa de bzhin te/chos zab mo 'di ma bshad kyi 'grib pa med la/bshad kyang 'phel pa med do//rab 'byor dper na de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa sangs 
7b7(khri pa): rgyas kyis ji srid 'tsho'i bar du nam mkha'i bsngags pa brjod na nam mkha' la ni bsngags pa brjod kyang 'phel ba med la/bsngags pa ma yin pa brjod kyang yongs su 'grib pa med do//
7b8(khri pa): rab 'byor de bzhin du sgyu ma'i skyes bu la ni bsngags pa brjod kyi 'phel ba med la bsngags pa ma yin pa brjodakyang yongs su 'grib pa maid do//rab 'byor de bzhin du chos de ni ma bshad kyang 
8a1(khri pa): @#//'grib pa med la/bshad kyang 'phel ba med do//bshad kyang de kho nar zad la ma bshad kyang de kho nar zad do//de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di 
8a2(khri pa): skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod cing shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la sgom pa ni mi zhum mi 'gong ste zhum par 
8a3(khri pa): 'gyur ba ma mchis la zhum pa ma mchis shing shes rab kyi pha rol tu yin pa 'di la yang snyoms par 'jug la/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las kyang phyir mi ldog pa ni dka' ba bgyid pa lags 
8a4(khri pa): so||de ci'i slad du zhe na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa bsgom pa 'di ni nam mkha' bsgom pa lags shing /sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i bar du yang de bzhin 
8a5(khri pa): te|nam mkha' la ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa ma mchis pa nas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyi pa'i bar du ma mchis/nam mkha' la ni gzugs ma mchis/tshor ba dang /'du 
8a6(khri pa): shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa ma mchis/nam mkha' la na nang stong pa nyid ma mchis pa nas phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa 
8a7(khri pa): nyid kyi bar du ma mchis/nam maka' la ni dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams ma mchis pa nas 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i bar du ma mchis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang /sangs 
8a8(khri pa): rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar du ma mchis/nas mkha' la ni rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu ma mchis/lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /dgra 
8b1(khri pa): bcom pa nyid dang /rang byang chub dang /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub ma mchis pa'i slad du'o//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag
8b2(khri pa): go cha chen po 'di 'tshal ba de dag ni phyag bgyi ba'i gnas lags so//btsun pa bcom ldan 'des gang dag sems can rnams kyi slad du go cha 'tshal ba de dag ni nam mkha'i slad du brtson par 'tshal ba dang /
8b3(khri pa): 'bad par 'tshal ba dang /rtsol bar 'tshal ba dag lags so//btsun pa bcol ldan 'das gang dag sems can rnam 'khor ba las thar par bgyi bar 'tshal zhing go cha chen po 'tshal ba de dag ni namamakha'
8b4(khri pa): yongs su thar par bgyi bar 'tshal ba lags so//btsun pa bcon ldan 'das gang dag semasacan rnams kyi slad du blon med pa yang dag par rtogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas
8b5(khri pa): pa bcom ldan 'das byang cub sems dpa' sems dpa'i chen po gang dag sems can rnamasakyi slad du bla na med pa yang dag par
8b6(khri pa): rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par zhugs pa de dag ni dpa' ba dang /rtul phod pa lags so//btsun pa bco ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag nam mkha' dang 'dra ba'i sems
8b7(khri pa): can rnams kyi slad du bla na med pa yag dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'tshal ba de dag ni go cha chen po 'tshal ba lags shing bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /mtshungs pa ma
9a1(khri pa): @#//mchis pa'i go cha 'tshal ba lags so//de ci'i slad du zhe na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das gal te stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di de bzhin 
9a2(khri pa): gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas sha stag gis dper bgyi na 'dam bu'i tshal lam/'od ma'i tshal lam/bu ram shing gi tshal lam/'bras sA lu'i zhing ngam/
9a3(khri pa): mda' rgyu'i tshal ltar yongs su bltam par gyur cing /de bzhin gshegs pa de dag kyang skal pa 'am/skal pa las lhag par bzhugs te chos bshad par gyur la/de bzhin gshegs pa re res 
9a4(khri pa): kyang sems can dpag tu ma mchis/grangs ma mchis/tshad ma mchis pa dag yongs su mya ngan las bzlas par gyur kyang /btsun pa bcom ldan 'da' sems can gyi khams 
9a5(khri pa): ni bri ba 'am/gang bi ma mngon lags so//de ci'i sladu zhe na/sems can ma mchis pa'i slad du dang /sems can dben pa'i slad du'o//de bzhin du gong ma bzhin du sbyar te/
9a6(khri pa): phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi bar du rgyas par sbyar bar bgyi ste/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das rnam grangs des na/'di skad du gang dag sems can rnams kyi slad du go cha chen po 'tshal 
9a7(khri pa): nas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'tshal ba de dag ni nam mkha' yongs su thar par 'tshal ba lags so//zhes mchis lags so//
9a8(khri pa): de nas 'khor de nyid na dge slong zhig 'di snyam du kye ma btsun pa bcom ldan 'das chos 'ga' yang mi skye mi 'gag kyang tshul khrims kyi phung po yang mchis/ting nge 'dzin gyi phung po dang /rnam par 
9b1(khri pa): grol ba'i phung bo dang /rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba'i phung po yang mchis shing /de bzhin du rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu yang mchis/lan cig phyir 'ong ba dang /phyir mi 'ong ba dang /
9b2(khri pa): dgra bcom pa dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom ba yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyang mchis/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yang mchis/
9b3(khri pa): sangs rgyas dkon mchog kyang mchis/chos dkon mchog kyang mchis /dge 'dun dkon mchog kyang mchis//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba yang mchis pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la phyag 'tshal 
9b4(khri pa): lo snyam mo//de nas lha'i dbang bo brgya byin gyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces smras so//btsun pa rab 'byor byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la rnal 'byor 
9b5(khri pa): du snyoms par 'jug pa de gang la rnal 'byor du snyoms par 'jug pa yin/tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis smras pa/gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa la rnal 'byor du snyoms 
9b6(khri pa): par 'jug par bya bar sems pa de ni nam mkha' la rnal 'byor du snyoms bar 'jug pa yin no//de nas bcom ldan 'das la lha'i dbang bo brgya byin kyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun ba bcom 
9b7(khri pa): ldan 'das rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di 'dzin pa dang /'chang ba dang /klog pa dang /kun chub par bgyid pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la bgyid pa de la 
9b8(khri pa): bdag gis srung ba dang /bskyab pa dang /sba ba ji lta bu zhig bgyi lags/de nas tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor kyi lha'i dbang po brgya byin la 'di skad ces smras so//kau shi ka khyod kyis gang 
10a1(khri pa): @#//la bsrung ba dang /bskyab pa dang /sba bar bya ba yang dag par rjes su mthong ngam/smras pa/btsun pa rab 'byor ma yin no//smras pa/kau shi ka gal te 
10a2(khri pa): rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo de dag ji skad bstan pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di la gnas na de nyid de dag gi bsrung ba dang /bskyabs pa dang /sba ba bya bar 'gyur te/de dag la mi dang 
10a3(khri pa): mi ma yin pa rnams kyis glags rnyed par mi 'gyur ro//rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang dag ji skad bstan pa'i she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin la gnas pa de dag shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa dang ma bral 
10a4(khri pa): bar rig par bya'o//kau shi ka gang zhig byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa la spyod pa dag la bsrung ba dang /bskya ba dang /sba bar bya 
10a5(khri pa): bar sems pa de ni nam mkha' la bsrung ba dang /bskye ba dang /sba bar bya bar sems pa yin no//kau shi ka khyod kyi rmi lam dang /smig rgyu dang /mig yor dang /sgyu ma dang /
10a6(khri pa): sprul pa la bsrung ba dang /bskyob pa dang /sba bar nus sam/smras pa/btsun pa rab 'byor ma yin no//kau shi ka de bzhin du gang zhig byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po shes rib kyi 
10a7(khri pa): pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa dag la bsung ba dang /bskyab pa dang /sba bar sems pin 'di lta ste mi 'grub pa dang ngal ba'i skal pa can du 'gyur ro//kau shi ka 'di ji snyam du sems/khyod 
10a8(khri pa): kyis de bzhin gshegs pa 'am/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sprul pa la bsrung ba dang/bskyab pa dang /sba ba nus sam/smras pa/btsum pa rab 'byor de ni mi yin no//kau shi ka 
10b1(khri pa): de bzhin du gang zhig byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa dag la bsrung ba dang/bskyab pa dang /sba bar bya bar sems pa de ni 'di lta ste mi 'grub pa dang /ngal 
10b2(khri pa): ba'i skal pa can du 'gyur bar zad do//kau shi ka 'di ji snyam du sems khyod kyis chos kyi dbyings dang /yang dag pa'i mtha' dang /bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dbyings la/bsrung ba dang /bskyab pa dang /
10b3(khri pa): sba bar nus sam/smras pa/btsun pa rab 'byor ma yin no//smras pa/kau shi ka de bzhin du gong ma bzhin du sbyar te 'di lta ste mi 'grub pa dad /ngal ba'i skal ba can du 'gyur bar zado//de nas 
10b4(khri pa): lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces smras so//btsun pa rab 'byor 'di ji snyam du sems/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po shes rab kyis pha rol tu 
10b5(khri pa): phyin pa la spyod pa na re shig rmi lam lta bu dang /smig rgyu lta bu dang /brag cha lta bu dang /sgyu ma lta bu dang /sprul pa lta bur chos rnams ji ltar yongs su shes pa yin/de shes nas 
10b6(khri pa): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rmi lam lta bur rlom sems su mi byed/rmi lam gyis rlom sems su mi byed pa yin/de skad ces smras pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis lha'i 
10b7(khri pa): dbang po brgya byin la 'di skad ces smras so//kau shi ka gang gi tshe byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa na gzugs su rlom sems su mi byed
10b8(khri pa): gzugs kyis rlom sems su mi byedgzugs kyi rlom sems su mi byed//gzugs la rlom sems su mi byed cing /de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes par 
11a1(khri pa): @#//rlom sems su mi byed/rnam par shes pas rlom sems su mi byed/rna bar shes pa'i rloms sems su mi byed/rnam bar shes pa la rlom sems su mi 
11a2(khri pa): byed la de bzhin du gong ma bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du rlom sems su mi byed/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i nyid kyi rlom sems su mi byed//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid kyi rlom 
11a3(khri pa): sems su mi byed/rnam pa thams cad mkhye ba nyid la rlom sems su mi byed pa de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pas/chos rnams rmi lam lta bu nas 
11a4(khri pa): chos rnams sprul pa lta bu'i bar du yongs su mi shes pa yin te/de shes nas rmi lam du rlom sems su mi byed/rmi lam gyis rlom sems su mi byedrmi lam gyi rlom sems su mi byed/
11a5(khri pa): rmi lam la rlom sems su mi byedapa nas sprul pa'i bar du rlom sems nas su mi byed/sprul pas rlom sems su mi byed/sprul ba'i rlom sems su mi byedsprul pa la yang rlom sems 
11a6(khri pa): su med pa'i phyir ro//de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di na rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha rnams dang /sum cu rtsa gsum pa rnams dang /'thab bral dang /
11a7(khri pa): dga' ldan dang /'phrul dga' dang /gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams dang /tshangs ris rnams dang /tshangs pa'i mdun na 'don rnams dang /tshangs 'khor rnams dang /tshangs ba rnams 
11a8(khri pa): dang /'od gsal gyi lha rnams dang /gnas gtsang ma'i bar gyi lha gang dag yod pa de dag thams cad kyis lha'i me tog dang /lha'i tsan dan gyi phye ma dag dang /spos kyi phye ma dag gis 
11b1(khri pa): bcom ldan 'das shA kya thub pa la gtor nas/bcom ldan 'das shA kya thab pa ga la ba de logs su dong ste/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//
11b2(khri pa): de nas rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha de dag dang /gnas gtsang ma'i bar gyis lha de dag gis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/shar phyogs nas sangs rgyas stong rnam pa 'di dag nyid kyis chos ston cing ming 'di zhes bya ba 'di lta 
11b3(khri pa): ste/dge slong rab 'byor ces bya ba sha stag shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di nyid yongs su zhu ba byed la/de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin sha stag shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa'i le'u 'di nyid yongs su zhu bar byed pa 
11b4(khri pa): dag mthong bar gyur to//de bzhin du lho phyogs dang /nub phyogs dang /byang phyogs dang /'og gi phyogs dang /steng gi phyogs dang /de bzhin du phyogs mtshams bzhi'i 
11b5(khri pa): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du sangs rgyas stong mthong bar gyur te/gong ma bzhin du sbyar te rgya cher thams cad du sbyar bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang 
11b6(khri pa): ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas 
11b7(khri pa): nas sa phyogs 'di nyid du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di nyid ston par 'gyur ro//skal pa bzang po'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyang 
11b8(khri pa): sa phyogs 'di nyid du 'byung bar 'gyur zhing shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di nyid ston par gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun 
12a1(khri pa): pa bcom ldan 'das rnam pa gang dag dang /rtag gang dag dang /mtshan ma gang dag gis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs 
12a2(khri pa): pa'i byang chub du mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di nyid ston par 'gyur lags/de skad ces gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor 
12a3(khri pa): la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po byams pa la bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sang rgyas nas/gzugs 
12a4(khri pa): rtag ces bya ba dang /mi rtag ces bya bar chos ston par mi 'gyur ro//gzugs bde zhes bya ba dang /sdug bsngal zhes bya bar chos ston par myi 'gyur ro//gzugs bdag ces bya 
12a5(khri pa): ba dang /bdag med ces bya bar chos ston bar mi 'gyur ro//gzugs stong zhes bya ba dang mi stong zhes bya bar chos ston bar ma 'gyur ro//gzugs mtshan ma yod ces bya 
12a6(khri pa): ba dang mtshan ma med ces bya bar chos stong par mi 'gyur ro//gzungs smon pa yod ces bya ba dang smon pa med ces bya bar chos stonabar mi 'gyur ro//gzug zhi zhes bya ba dang /ma zhi zhes 
12a7(khri pa): bya bar chos ston par mi gyur ro//gzugs dben zhes bya ba dang mi dben zhes bya bar chos ston pa mi 'gyur ro//gzugs kun nas nyon mongs zhes bya ba dang /rnam par byang zhes bya ba 
12a8(khri pa): chos ston par mi 'gyur ro //gzugs skye zhes bya ba dang 'gag ces bya bar chos ston mi 'gyur ro//gzugs dngos po yod ces bya ba dang dngos po med ces bya bar chos stob par mi 'gyur ro//
12b1(khri pa): gzugs gtsang zhes bya ba dang mi gtsang zhes bya bar chos ston par mi 'gyur ro//gzugs bcings shes bya ba dang grol zhes bya bar chos ston pa mi 'gyur ro//de gzugs ji lta ba bzhin du tshor ba dang /
12b2(khri pa): 'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa bcings shes bya ba dang 'grol zhes bya bar chos ston bar mi 'gyur ro//gzugs 'das shes bya ba dang ma 'ongs shes bya ba dang /da ltar byung 
12b3(khri pa): zhes bya bar chos ston par ma 'gyur ro//de bzhin du tshor ba dang 'du shes dang /'du byed ma rnams dang /rnam par shis pa 'das shes bya ba dang /ma 'ongs shes bya ba dang da ltar byung zhes bya bar 
12b4(khri pa): zhes bya bar chos ston mar mi 'gyur ro//de bzhin da sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du 'das shes bya ba dang ra chos ston par mi 'gyur//de bzhin du tshor ba ni/'du 
12b5(khri pa): shes dang 'du byed rnams dang rnam par shes pa shin tu rnam par dag ces bya ba dang /shin tu rnam par ma dag ces bya bar chos ston par mi 'gyur ro//de bzhinadu sbyar te rnam pa 
12b6(khri pa): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kya bar du shin tu rnam par dag cesabya ba dang /shin tu rnams par ma dag ces bya bar ches ston par mi 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 
12b7(khri pa): 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni yongs su dag pa lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor 
13a1(khri pa): @#//gzugs yongs su dag pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag pa yin no//rab 'byor de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /
13a2(khri pa): rnam par shes pa yongs su dag pa'i phyir zhes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin yongs su dag pa yin no //gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na gzugs yongs su dag pa'i sladu shes rab kyi pha 
13a3(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa yongs su ngag pa lags/de bzhin du tshor ba dang 'du shes dang /'du byed dang /rnam par shes pa yongs su/dag pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyen pa yongs su 
13a4(khri pa): dag pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor gzugs skye ba med pa dang /'gag pa med pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /rnam par byang 
13a5(khri pa): ba med pa'i phyir gzugs yongs su dag pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang mdu shes dang /'du byed nams dang /rnam par shes skye ba med pa dang 'gag pa med pa dang 
13a6(khri pa): kun nas nyon mongs pa medapa dang /rnam par byang ba med pa'i phyir rnam par shes pa yongs su dag pa yin no//rab 'byor gzhan yang nam mkha' yongs su dag pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs 
13a7(khri pa): su dag pa yin no//gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ji ltar nam mkha' yongs su dag ba'i slad du shes rab kyi pha ril tu phyin pa yongs su dag pa lags/bcom ldana'adas kyis bka' 
13a8(khri pa): stsal pa/rab 'byor nam mkha' la skye ba med pa dang /'gal pa med pa dang /kun na nyon mongs pa med pa dag rnam par byang ba med pa'i phyer nam mkha' yongs su da pa yin no//
13b1(khri pa): rab 'byor gzhan yang gzugs gos pa med pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag pa yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa 
13b2(khri pa): gos pa med pa'i phyir shes rim kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yos su dag pa yin no/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na nam mkha' gos pa ma mchis pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi 
13b3(khri pa): pa yongs su dag pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor nam mkha' la bzung du med pa'i phyir shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs gsu dag pa lags/de bzhin du nam mkha' 
13b4(khri pa): la tha snyad gdags su med pa'i phyir shes rab kya pha rol tu phyin pa yongs du dag pa yin no//rab 'byor dper na nam mkha' la brag cha gnyis kyi sgra grags pa de bzhin du rab 'byor nam mkha' 
13b5(khri pa): la tha snyad gags su med pa'i phyir shes rim gyi pha rol tu phyin pa na pa yongs su dag pa yi no/gsol pa/btsun ba bcom ldan 'das ji ltar ma mkha' dmigs su ma mchis 
13b6(khri pa): pa'i slad du shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag pa lags bcom ldan 'das kyis bka stsal pa/rab 'byor namamakha' la ni ci yang dmigs su med pa'i phyir shes reb kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 
13b7(khri pa): yongs su dag pa yin no//rab byor gzhan yang chos thams cad skye ba med pa dang /'gag pa med pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /rnam par byang ba med pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha 
13b8(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa yongs su ga ba yin no//gsol ba/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na chos thams cad skye ba ma mchis pa dang /'gag pa ma mchis pa dang /kun nas nyon
14a1(khri pa): mongs pa ma mchis pa dang /rnam par byang ba ma mchis pa'i slad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/
14a2(khri pa): rab 'byor chos thams cad shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phin pa yongs su dag pa yin no//de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//
14a3(khri pa): btsun pa bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa 'di 'dzin pa dang /'chang ba dang /klog pa dang /kun chub par bgyid pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid 
14a4(khri pa): la bgyid pa de la ni/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das mig nad 'byung bar mi 'gyur/rna ba'i nad 'byung bar mi 'gyur /sna nad 'byung bar mi 'gyur/lce nad 'byung bar 
14a5(khri pa): mi 'gyur/yan lag ma tshang bar mi 'gyur/lus ha cang 'khogs par mi 'gyur gshis ngan du 'gum par mi 'gyur lags so//'di lta ste rgyal chen bzhi'i 
14a6(khri pa): ris kyi lha rnams nas gnas gtsang ma'i bar gyi lha dag dang /lha stong phrag mang po dag kyang de'i slad du 'brang bar 'gyur lags so//tshes brgyad dang /tshes bcu bzhi dang /tshes bco lnga 
14a7(khri pa): la gang na rigs kyi bu 'am rigs gyi bu mo chos ston pa de shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di kha ton du bgyid pa dang /'chad par lha'i tshogs mang po 'chis bar 'gyur zhing bsod nams dpag tu 
14a8(khri pa): ma mchis grangs ma mchis/tshad ma mchis/bsam gyis mi khyab/gzhal du ma mchis pa mang po dag kyang skye bar 'gyur lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 
14b1(khri pa): 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor de de bzhin te/ji skad smras pa de bzhin te/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo tshes brgyad dang /tshes bcu bzhi 
14b2(khri pa): dang /tshes bco lnga la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di kha don du byed pa dang /'chad par byed pa na bsod nams kyi phung po dpag tu med/grangs med/tshad med/bsam gyis mi 
14b3(khri pa): khyab/gzhal du med pa mang po dag bskyed par 'gyur zhing /de shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di kha don du byed ba dang /'chad par byed na lha'i 'khor mdun du gnas par 'gyur ro//de ci'i 
14b4(khri pa): phyir zhe na/rab 'byor 'di lta ste shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa 'di ni rin po che yin pa'i phyir te/rab 'byor de la rin po che ni 'di yin te/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi
14b5(khri pa): pa ni sems can dmyal ba'i 'gro ba las yongs su thar par byed pa dang /dud 'gro'i skye gnas dang /gshen rje'i 'jig rten dang /mi dbul po dang /
14b6(khri pa): gnod sbyin dbul po dang /klu dbul po las yongs su thar bar byed pa yin no//gzhan yang rgyal rigs shing sA la chen po lta bu rnams sbyin par byed pa dang /de bzhin du bram ze shing 
14b7(khri pa): sA la chen po lta bu rnam dang /khyim bdag shing sA la chen po lta ba rnams dang /tshong dpon shing sA la lta bu rnams sbyin par bye ba dang /de bzhin du rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha rnams kyi nang du 
14b8(khri pa): skye ba sbyin par byed pa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te 'du shes med 'du shes med min skye mched kyi lha rnams kyi nang du skye ba sbyin par byed pa dang/de bzhin du rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu sbyin par byed 
15a1(khri pa): @#//pa dang /de bzhin du lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /dgra bcom pa nyid dang /rang byang chub dang /bla na med pa yang dag par 
15a2(khri pa): rdzogs pa'i byang chub sbyin par byed pa yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'dir dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam rnams rgya cher bstan te/de la bslabs nas rgyal rigs shing 
15a3(khri pa): sA la chen po lta bu dag 'jig rten na yod pa dang /de bzhin du bram ze rigs shing sA la chen po lta bu dag dang /khyim bdag shing sA la chen po lta budag dang /tshong dpon shing 
15a4(khri pa): sA la chen po lta ba dag dang /de bzhin du rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha rnams yod pa nas gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha rnams kyi bar dag 'jig rten na yod dang /de bzhin du tshangs ris 
15a5(khri pa): kyi lha rnams dang /tshangs pa'i mdun na 'don pa rnams dang /tshangs 'khor rnams dang /tshangs pa chen po rnams dang /'od chen rnams dang /'od chung rnams dang /tshad med 
15a6(khri pa): 'od rnams dang /'od gsal rnams dang /dge ba rnams dang /dge chung rnams dang /tshad me dge rnams dang /dge rgyas rnams dang /'du shes med 'du shes med min skye mched kyi 
15a7(khri pa): bar gyi lha rnams 'jig rten na yod pa dang/de bzhin du rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu yod pa dang /de bzhin du lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang /phyir mi 'ongs ba'i 'bras bu dang /dgra 
15a8(khri pa): bcom pa dang /rang byang chub dang /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'jig rten na yod par 'gyur ba dang /rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa de la ni bsam gtan bzhi stan 
15b1(khri pa): pa yod pa dang /de bzhin du 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i bar du bstan pa yod pa dang /de bzhinadu sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa bstan pa yod pa dang /de bzhin du tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu 
15b2(khri pa): phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bstanapa yod pa dang /de bzhin
15b3(khri pa): nang stong pa nyid bstan pa dang /phyi stong pa nyid bstan pa dang/phyi nang stong pa nyid bstan pa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pha nyid kyi bar du bstan pa yod ba dang /de bzhin du de
15b4(khri pa): bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu bstan pa dang /gong ma bzhi na du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pi nyad kyi bar du bstan pa yod de/de la bslabs nas de bzhin gshegs pa 
15b5(khri pa): dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams 'jig rten yod pa'i phyir te/des na 'di lta ste shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na 'di ni rin po che'i pha rol tu phyin pa

23b6(khri pa): lta bu yin yang de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi don du ming dang /brda' dang /gdags pa dang /tha snyad kyi tha snyad du 'jogs par mdzad de/lha dag de dag gi don dam pa ma yin te/lha dang

23b8(khri pa): nyid kyis mtshan nyid rab tu mi shes/mtshan nyid kyis mtshan nyid med pa yang rab tu mi shes/mtshan nyad med pas mtshan nyid rab tu mi shos/mtshan nyid med pas mtshan nyid med pa yang tab tu

25a1(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di ngo mtshar lags so//chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid thugs su chud pa ni gzugs dang /tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du

28a2(khri pa): ngo zhes brjod do//de bzhIn du 'jig rten rnams las dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi stong ngo zhes brjod do/de bzhin du gong ma bzhin du sbyar te/'jig rten rnams la sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar 
28a3(khri pa): du stong ngo zhes brjod do/'jig rten rnams la rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid stong ngo zhes brjod/rab 'byor de ltar na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa de bzhin gshegs pa rnams bskyed pa dang /'jig rten 
28a4(khri pa): rnams ston par byed pa yin no//rab 'byor gzhan yang de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di nyid la brten nas 'jig rten rnams 
28a5(khri pa): la phung po lnga stong ngo zhes ston to//'jig rten yang stong ngo zhes khong du chud cing rig la/kun du shes shing 'jig rten na dag la yang de kho na bzhin du brjod par byed do//rab 'byor 
28a6(khri pa): de ltar na shes rab kyi pha ril tu phyin pa de bzhin gshes pa rnams bskyed pa dang 'jig rten rnams ston par byed pa yis na no/rab 'byor gzhan yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di de bzhin gshegs pa 
28a7(khri pa): dgra bcom pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la 'jig rten stong ngo zhes ston par byed do//'jig rten stong par ston pa de gang zhe na/phung po lnga'i 'jig rten de stong par ston to//de bzhin du 
28a8(khri pa): skye mched bcu gnyis kyi 'jig rten de stong par stod to//de bzhin du khams bco brgyad kyi 'jig rten de stong pa ston to//de bzhin du gong ma bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar gyi 'jig rten 
28b1(khri pa): de stong par ston par byed do//rab 'byor de ltar na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams bskyed pa dang /'jig rten rnams ston par byed pa rnams yin no//rab 'byor gzhan 
28b2(khri pa): yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la 'jig rten rnams bsam gyis mi khyab par ston par byed do/'jig rten gang zhig bsam gyis 
28b3(khri pa): mi khyab pa de gang yin zhe na/'di lta ste phung po lnga dang /skye mched bcu gnyis dang /khams bco brgyad kyi 'jig rten bsam gyis mi khyab par stor to//de bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad 
28b4(khri pa): mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar gyi 'jig rten bsam gyis mi khab pa ston to//rab 'byor de ltar na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di de bzhin gshegs rnams bskyed pa dang //'jig rten rnams ston 
28b5(khri pa): par byed pa yin no//rab 'byor gzhar yang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di de bzhin gshegs dgra bcom pa yang dag dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs/rgyas rnams la 'jig rten zhi zhes bya 
28b6(khri pa): ba dang /ngo bo nyid kyis stong zhes bya bar chos ston to//'jig rten zhi zhes bya ba nas stong zhes bya ba'i bar du ston par byed pa de gang zhe na/'di lta ste phung bo lnga nas rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi 
28b7(khri pa): bar du zhi zhes bya bar ston to//de bzhin du phung po lnga nas/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen nyid kyi bar du ngo bo nyid kyis stong zhes bar ston to//rab 'byor de ldar na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 
28b8(khri pa): 'di de bzhin gshegs pa rnams bskyed pa dang /'jig rten rnams ston par byed pa yin no//raba' 'byor gzhan yang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di de bzhin gshes pa dgra bcom pa yang dag
29a1(khri pa): @#//par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la 'jig rten 'di la 'jig rten du 'du shes med pa dang /'jig rten pha rol la 'jig rten du 'du she med par 'jig rten gyi 'du shes ston par 
29a2(khri pa): byed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor 'di ltar chos gang dag gis 'jig rten 'dir shes pa 'am/'jig rten pha rol tu 'du shes par 'gyur ba'i chos rnams med cing dmigs su med pa'i phyir ro//de skad ces 
29a3(khri pa): bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni mdzad pa chen pos nye bar gnas pa lags so//
29a4(khri pa): btsun pa bcom ldan 'das/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di ni mdzad pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /mdzad pa dpag tu mchis pas nye bar gnas pa lags swo//bcom ldan 'das kyis 
29a5(khri pa): bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor de de bzhin no//ji skad smra sa pa de bzhin te/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di ni mdzad pas nye bar gnas pa yin zhing /de bzhin du 
29a6(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin/phyin pa 'di ni mdzad pa bsams gyis mi khyab pa dang //mdzad pa gzhal du med pa dang /mdzad pa mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pas nye bar gnas pa yin//rab 'byor ji ltar na shes rab 
29a7(khri pa): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'dis ni mdzad pa chen po dang /mdzad pa mi mnyan pa dang mnyam pa'i bar gyi nye bar gnas pa yin zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
29a8(khri pa): rnams kyi mdzad pa chen po ni 'di yin te/'di lta ste sems can thams cad bskyab par bya ba'i don dang /yongs su mi btang bar bya ba'i don du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di mdzad pa chen pos 
29b1(khri pa): nye bar gnas pa yin no//rab 'byor ji ltar na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di mdzad pa bsam gyis mi khyab pas nye bar gnas pa yin zhe na/rab 'byor bsam gyis mi khyab pa ni 'di lta ste yang dag 
29b2(khri pa): par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nyid dang /de bzhin gshegs pa nyid dang /rang byung nyid dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yin te/rab 'byor rnam grangs 'dis kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di 
29b3(khri pa): de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi mdzad pa bsam gyis mi khyad pas nye bar gnas pa yin/rab 'byor ji ltar na shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i mdzad pa 
29b4(khri pa): gzhal du med pas nye bar gnas pa yang zhe na/rab 'byor 'gro ba lnga po dag na sems can du sdu bar bsdus pa la gang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i /sangs rgyas nyid dam/de bzhin gshegs pa nyi 
29b5(khri pa): dam/rang byung nyid dam/thams cad mkhyen pa nyi bas ma pa'am/gzhal bar nus pa'i sems can ni 'ga' yang med la/de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yin pa'i mthu yin 
29b6(khri pa): te/rab 'byor rnam grangs nges na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa 'di de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcam pa yang dag par rdzogs na de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams mdzad pa gzhal du med pas nye bar bzung ba gnas pa yin no//
29b7(khri pa): rab 'byor ji ltar na shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di mdzad pa dpag tu med pas nye bar gnas na yin zhe na/rab 'byor dpag tu med pa ni 'di lta ste yang dag par rdzogs pa sangs rgyas nyid dang /de 
29b8(khri pa): bzhin gshegs pa nyid dang /rang byung nyid dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yin te/rab 'byor 'gro ba lnga po dag na sems can du bsdu bar bsdus pa la gang de bzhin gshegs pa dag 
30a1(khri pa): bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams dang sems mnyam pa'i sems can ni 'ga' yang med na lhag pa lta ga la yod de/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 
30a2(khri pa): bcom pa yang dag par rdzog pa'i sangs rgyas de dag kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas rab tu phye ba yin te/rab 'byor rnam grangs des na shes rab kyi pha rol tu pyin pa 'di de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa 
30a3(khri pa): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sadas rgyas rnams kyi mdzad pa dpag tu med pa nas mdzad pa mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bar gyis nye bar gnas pa yin no//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la tshe 
30a4(khri pa): dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ci yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
30a5(khri pa): sangs rgyas nyid dang /de bzhin gshegs pa nyid dang /rang byung nyid dang /
30a6(khri pa): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /gzhan la du ma mchis pa dang /dpag tu ma mchis pa dang /mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa lags sam/bcom ldan 'das 
30a7(khri pa): kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor de de bzhin no//ji skad smras pa de bzhin te/rab 'byor 'di ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa yin te/'di lta ste yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sems rgyas nyid dang /
30a8(khri pa): de bzhin gshegs pa nyid dag/rang byung nyid dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yin no//rab 'byor 'di ni gzhal du med pa yin te/'di lta ste yag dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nyid dang /
30a9(khri pa): de bzhan gshegs pa nyid dang / rang byung nyid dang /tham cad mkhyen pa nyid yin na/rab 'byor 'di ni dpag tu med pa yin te/'di ltar ste yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas nyid dang /
30b1(khri pa): pa nyid dang /rang byung nyid dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yin no//rab 'byor 'di ni mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa yin te/'di lta ste yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nyid dang /de bzhin 
30b2(khri pa): gshegs pa nyid dang /rang byung gnyid dang /thams cad mkhye ba nyid yin no//rab 'byod de bzhin du gzugs kyang bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /gzhal du med pa dang /dpag tu med pa dang /
30b3(khri pa): mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa yan no//rab 'byor de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa yang bsam gyis mi khyab pa la dang /gzhal du med pa'i dang /dpag 
30b4(khri pa): tu med pa dang /mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pas yin no//rab 'byor de bzhIn du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du yang bsam gyis mi khyab nas mi mnyam pa dang mnyam ba'i bar 
30b5(khri pa): du yin no//rab 'byor chos thams cad kyang bsam gyis mi khya/ba pa nas mi mnyam pa dang ma nyam pa'i bar du yin te/de dag la ni sems dang sems las byung ba'i chos rnams med cing 
30b6(khri pa): mi dmigs so//rab 'byor gzugs kyang bsam gyis ma khyab pa nas mi mnyam pa dag mnyam pa'i bar du yin te med do//rab 'byor de bzhin tu tshor ba dang /'du shes/'du byed rnams dang 
30b7(khri pa): rnam par shes pa yang bsami gyis mi khyab pa nas mi mnyam pa'i bar du yin ste med do//rab 'byor debazhin du sbyar te rnam pa thamasacad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar da yang bsam gyis mi khyab pa nas mi nyams 
30b8(khri pa): pa dang mnyam pa'i bar du yin nate med do//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du gzugs 
31a1(khri pa): @#//bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /gzhal du ma mchis pa dang /dpag tu ma mchis pa dang /mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bar du lags te ma mchis pa lags//de bzhin 
31a2(khri pa): du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang/gzhal du ma mchis pa dag/dpag tu ma mchis pa bas/mi mnyam pa dang mnyam ba'i 
31a3(khri pa): bar du lags te ma mchisapa lags/de bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du bsam gyis mi khyab pa nas mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa ni bar du lags te ma mchi pa lags/bcom 
31a4(khri pa): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor gyis gzugs la yang tshad med de/de'i phyir gzugs bsam gyis mi khyab pa na mi mnyam pa dang mnyam ba dang mnyam pa'i bar du yin te med do//
31a5(khri pa): rab 'byor de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du she dang /'du byed rnams rnams dang /rnam par she pa yang tshad med de/de'i/phyir rnam par shes pa yang bsam gyis mi khyab pa ni mi mnyam 
31a6(khri pa): pa dang mnyam pa'i bar du 'du byed yin te med do//rab 'byor de bzhin du sbyar te rnam thams cad mkhyen pa nyidakyi bar du yang tshad med de/der phyir rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa na
31a7(khri pa): mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bar duyin te med do//gsol pa/ci'i slad du gzugs la tshad ma mchis pa lags/de bzhin du tshor ba dang 'du shes dang 'du byed rnams dang /rnam 
31a8(khri pa): par shes pa tshad ma mchis pa lags de bzhin du sbyar te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du tshad ma mchis pa lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor 
31b1(khri pa): gzugs bsam gyis mi khyab pa nas mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bar du yin te/de'i phyir gzugs la tshad med do//rab 'byor de bzhin du gong ma bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar 
31b2(khri pa): du bsam gyis mi khyab pa na mi mnyam pa dang mnyam ba'i bar du yin te/de'i phyer rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid kyi bar du tshad med do//rab 'byong 'di ji snyam du sems/bsam gyis mi khyab pa nas
31b3(khri pa): mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i par gang yin pa de la gzugs dmigs su yod snyam 'am/de bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa mtha'as cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi par du dmigs su yod snyam 'am gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 
31b4(khri pa): 'das ma mchis lags so//rab 'byor rnam grangs des na chos thams cad bsam gyis mi khyab pa nasami mnyam pa dang mnyam pa 'i bar du yin no//rab 'byor 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs 
31b5(khri pa): pa'i chos rnams yin te/'di lta ste/bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /gzhal du med pa dang /dpag tu med pa dang /mi mnyam pa dang mnyam ste/de'i phyir de 
31b6(khri pa): bzhin gshegs pa'i chos rnams ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa/bsam pa dang bral ba/gzhal du med pa/gzhal ba dang bral ba/dpag tu med pa/dpag pa dang bral ba/mi mnyam pa dang 
31b7(khri pa): mnyam pa med pa ste/mi mnyam pa dang mnyamapa dang bral ba yin no//rab 'byor bsam gyis mi khyab pa zhes bya ba de ni/bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i tshig bla dwags yin no//gzhal du 
31b8(khri pa): med pa zhes bya ba de ni gzhal du med pa'i tshig bla dwags yin/dpag tu med pa zhes bya ba de ni dpag tu med pa'i tshig bla dwagasayin no//rab 'byor rnam grangs des kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos rna
32a1(khri pa): @#//ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa nas mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bar du yin no//rab 'byor dper na nam mkha' ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang mi mnyam pa dang mnyam ba yin no//rab 'byor de bzhin du de bzhin 
32a2(khri pa): gshegs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos thams cad ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa mi mnyam pa dang mnyam yin te/de dag kyang lha dang /mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis bsam pa 'am gzhal ba 
32a3(khri pa): 'am/'grang bar mi nus so//de bzhin du dpag tu med pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i de bzhin gsheg pa'i chos rnams yin no//de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos rnams bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang/gzhal du 
32a4(khri pa): med pa dang /dpag tu med pa dang /mi mnyam pa dang mnyam    pa'i le'u 'di bshad pa na/dge slong lnga brgya ni len pa med par zag pa rnams las sems rnam par grol lo//dge slong ma nyi brgya ni len 
32a5(khri pa): pa med par zag pa rnams pas sems rnam par grol//dge bsnyen     drug brgya dang /dge bsnyen ma sum brgya ni chos kyi mig rdul     med cing dri ma dang bral ba skyes so//byang chub sems dpa' 
32a6(khri pa): nyis stong gis ni mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob ste de dag kyang skal pa bzang po 'di nyid la lung bstan to////shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa khri pa las/yon tan gyi le'u 
32a7(khri pa): zhes bya ste nyi shu pa rdzogas+ho////shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa khri pa//bam po nyi shu pa//de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom 
32a8(khri pa): ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyinapa 'di ni zab pa lags shing /btsun pa bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha ralatu phyin pa 'di ni bgyi ba chen pos nye bar gnas pa yin lags so//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das
32b1(khri pa): de bzhin du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni bgyi ba gzhal du ma mchis pa dang /bgyi ba dpag tu ma mchis pa dang /mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bgyi bas gnas pa lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /
32b2(khri pa): bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor de de bzhin no//ji skad smras pa de bzhin te/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di ni bya ba chen pos nye bar gnas pa nas/
32b3(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyind pa 'di ni mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa ba'i bar gyis nye bar gnas pa yang yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'dir pha rol tu phyin pa lnga 'dus pa dang /nang stong 
32b4(khri pa): pa nyid 'dus pa nas dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid kyi bar du 'dus pa dang /dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi 'dus pa dang /de bzhin du sbyar te 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i bar du 'dus pa dang /
32b5(khri pa): rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'dir/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu 'dus pa dang /rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa 'dir sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad 
32b6(khri pa): kyi bar du 'dus pa'i phyir ro//rab 'byor dper na rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur ba yul gyi mi la spyod pa thob pa'i rgyal po'i dgos pa dang /rgyal po'i bya ba gang dag yin pa de dag thams cad ni bka' la gtogs 
32b7(khri pa): pa'i mi la yod cing blon po chen po'i mi la yod pa yin te/rgyal po ni bya ba de dag la snying las chung ba dang khur bor ba yin no//rab 'byor de bzhin du nyan thos kyi chos rnams sam/rang sangs rgyas 
32b8(khri pa): kyi chos rnams sam/byang chub sems dpa'i chos rnams sam/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos gang dag ci yod pa de dag thams cad ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapar 'dus shing /
33a1(khri pa): @#//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ni de dag gi bya ba byed pa yin no//rab 'byor de lta bas na rnam grangs des na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di ni bya ba chen pos nye bar gnas 
33a2(khri pa): pa yin zhing /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinas pa 'di ni mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i bya ba'i bar dang /gzugs la yongs su 'dzin pa med cing mngon par zhen pa med pa dang /de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du 
33a3(khri pa): shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa la yongs su 'dzin pa medacing mngon par zhen pa medapa dang /de bzhin du gong ma bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar la yongs su 'dzin
33a4(khri pa): pa med cing mngon par zhe na pa med pa nye bar gnas pa yin no//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan

33a8(khri pa): ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor ci khyod kyis gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas yongs su bzung ba 'am mngon par zhen par bya ba'i gzugs de yang dag par rjes su mthong ngam/gsol pa/btsun pa 
33b1(khri pa): bcom ldan 'das ma lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor ci khyod kyis gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas yongs su bzung ba 'am mngon par zhen par bya ba'i tshor ba de dang /'du shes 
33b2(khri pa): de dang /'du byed de dag dang /rnam par shes pa de yang dag par rjes su mthong ngam/gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das ma lags swo//bcom ldan 'das kyis gaka' stsal pa/rabyor 
33b3(khri pa): ci khyod kyis gang shes rabakyi pha rol tu phyin pas yong su bzung ba 'am/mngon par zhen par bya ba rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar de yang dag par rjes sum mthong ngam/gsol pa//btsun pa bcom

33b5(khri pa): ma mthong /de bzhin du ngas kyang gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi ma lo su gzung ba ni mngon par zhen par bya ba'i tshor ba de dang /'du shes de dang /'du byed de dag dang /rnam par shes pa de yang dag par rjes su ma mthong ngo //

45a6(khri pa): ci rtsom yang rung /bsam gtan gang dag ci la snyoms par 'jug kyang rung /shes rab gang ci bsgom yang rung de dag thams cad sems can tham sa cad dang thun mong du byas la de yang dmigs pa'i tshul gyis bla ma med pa

45b8(khri pa): rol tu phyin pa 'di yang dag par ston par bgyid pa dag dang /de bzhin du bsam gtan gyi phar rol tu phyin pa dang //brtson 'grus kyi pha rol du phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyi pa dang /tshul

46b7(khri pa): rab 'byor gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'dir yang dag par bstan pa la slob par 'dod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de la byang chub sems dpa' 'di skad ces 'doms shing rjes su ston te/
46b8(khri pa): kye rigs kyi bu khyod shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na zab mo 'di la spyod pa ni gzugs la 'dod pa ma bskyed cig/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa la 'dod pa

55b8(khri pa): la mngon par yid ches shing mos pa 'gyur ba de dag ji lta bu'i tshul can du 'gyur ba lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor byang chub semasadapa' sems dpa' chen po gang dag she rab kyi pha rol

56a2(khri pa): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dag 'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa yongs su 'dzin pa ma mchis pa'i bsgom pa dag ni thams cad mkhyen ba nyid kyi tshul can gyi tshul du 'gyur lags so//
56a3(khri pa): btsun pa bcom ldan 'dasa'adi lta ste/shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa'i chos thams cad rnam par 'jig pa lags na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'da' chos thams cad rnam par

56a5(khri pa): 'di lta ste shes rab kyi phar rol tu 'byin pa bsgom pa de ni gzugs sa dang /tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa dang /nang gi skye mched

56b8(khri pa): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa'i de ni tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa rnam par 'jig pa yin no//'di lta ste shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa bsgom 
57a1(khri pa): @#//pa de ni mig rnam par 'jig pa yin no//de bzhin du 'di lta ste shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa de ni rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid rnam 
57a2(khri pa): par 'jig pa yin no//'di lta ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa bsgom pa de ni gzugs rnam par 'jig pa yin la/de bzhin du 'di lta ste she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin bsgom pa de ni sgra dang /dri dang /
57a3(khri pa): ro dang /reg bya dang /chos rnams rnam par 'jig pa yin//'di lta ste shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa bsgom pa'i de ni mig gi khams rnam par 'jig pa yino//de bzhin du 'di ltar ste shes rab kyi 
57a4(khri pa): pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom de ni gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams rnam par 'jig pa'i las dang /'di lta ste shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa bsgom pa 'di ni rna ba'i 
57a5(khri pa): khams dang /sgra'i khams dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /rnam par 'jig pa yin no//de lta ste shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bsgoms pa de ni sna'i khams dang /

57a7(khri pa): pa'i khams rnam par 'jigs pa yin no//'di lta ste shes rab kyang pha rol tu phyin pa bsgom/de ni lus kyi khams dang /reg bya'i khams dang /lus kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams ma rnam par 'jig pa 
57a8(khri pa): yin no//'di lta ste she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin bsgom pa de ni yid kyi khams dang /chos kyi khams dang /yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i kha nas rnam par 'jig pa yin//'di lta ste/

57b3(khri pa): bsgom pa de ni sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar du rnam par 'jig pa yon no//de bzhin du 'di lte ste shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa bsgom pa de ni thams cad shes pa nyid dang /lam gyi rnam pa shes 
57b4(khri pa): pa nyid dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid rnam par 'jig pa yin no //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rab 'byor shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa zab 
57b5(khri pa): mo 'dir sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang /tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dag sa phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa' nye bar brtag par bya 
57b6(khri pa): ste|byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po la la ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di la mngon par zhen par mi byed do//de bzhin du bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang brtson 'grus 
57b7(khri pa): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyinapa dang /tshul khrims kyi phar rol tu phyin pa dang /sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin la mngon par zhen par ma byed do//de bzhin du sbyar te rnam pa thaMd 
58a1(khri pa): @#//mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar la mngon par zhen par mi byed do//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po la la ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di la spyod pa gzhan gyis 
58a2(khri pa): smras pa'i tshig dag la snying po med pa dang /de kho na ma yin par khong du chud par byed de gzhan gyi dad pas mi 'gro'o//'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug dang /mtshungs par ldan pa'i sems 
58a3(khri pa): dang /sems las byung ba'i nye ba'i nyon mongs pa rnams kyis 'phrog par mi 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa' chen po la ni shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa zab mo 'di las spyod pa ni sbyin pa'i pha rolatu 
58a4(khri pa): phyin pa dang bral bar mi 'gyur ro//tshul khrims kyi pha rolatu phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rolatu phyin pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rolatu phyin pa dang/bsam gtan gyi pha rolatu phyin pa dang 
58a5(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyinapa dang bral bar mi 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa'-i sems dpa' chen po la shis rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa zab mo 'di la spyod pan shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin

58b5(khri pa): rdzogs pa'i byang chub las kyang yid phyir ldog par mi 'gyur ba'i steng du shes yab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'di 'jin par bgyid pa nas tshul bzhin du dal bgyid pa'i bar du bgyid pa'i byang chub
58b6(khri pa): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po des shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i 'di la ji ltar spyad par bgyi ba lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la gzhol ba'i sems kyi
58b7(khri pa): rgyud kyis spyad par bya'o//gsol pa/'di ltar rnam pa thams cad mkhyen nyid la gzhol ba'i semasakyi rgyud kyis spyad par bgyi lags/bcom ldan 'dasakyis bka' stsal pa/stong pa nyid
58b8(khri pa): la gzhol ba'i sems kyis rgyud dang /de bzhin du mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la gzhol ba'i sems kyi rgyud kyis spyad par bya'o/de bzhin du nam mkha' la gzhol ba'i sems kyi brgyud dang /de
59a1(khri pa): bzhin du sbyar te skye ba med pa dang /'gag pa med pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /rnam par byang ba med pa la gzhol pa'i sems kyi rgyud dang /de

59b6(khri pa): bam/de bzhin du sbyar te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen na pa'i ye shes kyi bar gyis kyang mngon par rdzogs bar 'tshang rgya bar mi nus so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gzugs nyid rnam 
59b7(khri pa): pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yin la/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /de bzhin du 'du shes dang /de bzhin du 'du byed rnams dang /de bzhin du rnam par shes pa nyid rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nying yin pa'i 
59b8(khri pa): phyir ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor 'di ltar gzugs kyi de bzhin nyid gang yin pa dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid kyi de bzhin nyid gang yinapa'i de bzhin nyid de ni gcig pa nyid yin/de bzhin du 
60a1(khri pa): @#//tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa'i de bzhin nyid gang yin pa dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi de bzhin nyid gang yin ba'i

60a3(khri pa): de bzhin nyid de ni gagacig pa nyid yin/de bzhIn du thams cad shes pa nyid kyi de bzhin nyidagang yin pa dang /lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid dang /rnam pa thams cang mkhyen pa nyid kyi de bzhIn nyid gang yin pa'i

60a5(khri pa): de nas 'dod pa na spyod pa dang /gzugs na spyod pa'i lha dag gis lha'i tsan dan gyi phye ma dag dang /rgya spos dang /ta ma la'i lo ma'i phye ma dag dang /lha'i ud pa la dang /

60b5(khri pa): pa'i de bzhin nyid de ni gcig pa nyid de gnyis su ma mchis shi/nga /gnyis su dbyer ma mchis so//'di ltar mig nyid rnam pa thams cad ma khyen pa nyid lags la rnam pa thams cad 
60b6(khri pa): mkhyen pa nyid mig lags so//de bzhin du rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid nyid rnam pa thams cad 'khyen pa nyid lags la/rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid yid lags te/
60b7(khri pa): mig gi de bzhin nyid gang lags dang /rnam pa thams can mkhyen pa nyid kyi de bzhinanyid gang lags pa'i de bzhin nyid de ni gcag pa nyid de gnyisu ma mchis shing gnyisu dbyer ma mchis so/de bzhin

61a1(khri pa): @#//de gnyis su ma mchis shing gnyis su dbyer ma mchis so//'di ltar gzugs rnams nyid rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid lags la rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid gzugs

61a4(khri pa): so//de bzhin du mig gi khams nyid rnam pa thams can mkhyen pa nyid la gas la rnam pa thams can mkhyenapa nyid mig gi khams lags te/mig gi de bzhin nyid gang lags ba dang /rnam pa 
61a5(khri pa): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi de bzhin nyid gag lags pa'i de bzhin nyid de na gcig pa nyid de gnyis gsum mchis shing gnyis su dbyer ma mchis so//'di ltar gzugs kyi khams dang /mig

61a8(khri pa): dang /yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i khamams nyid rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid lags la rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i lam lags te/de ltar na yid kyi rnam par shes pa' 
61b1(khri pa): khams kyi de bzhin nyid gang lags pa dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi de bzhin nyid gang la gas pa'i de bzhin nyid de ni gcig pa nyid de gnyis su ma mchis shing gnyis su dbyer ma mchis so//de bzhin 
61b2(khri pa): du 'di ltar dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams nyid rnam pa thams cad mkhyen nyid lags la rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid dran pa nye bar gzhag rnams lags so//de bzhin du yang dag par spong ba gang dag lags pa dang /
61b3(khri pa): rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams dang /dbang po rnams dang /stobs rnams dang /byang chub kyi yan lag rnams dang /de bzhin du sbyar te 'di ltar 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa nyid rnam pa thams cad
61b4(khri pa): mkhyen pa nyid lags la rnam pa tham sa cad mkhyen pa nyid 'phags pa'i lam yan la brgyad pa lags te/de ltar na 'phags pa'i lam brgyad pa'i de bzhin gang lags pa dang /rnamapa thams cad
61b5(khri pa): mkhyen pa nyidakyi de bzhin nyid gang lags pa'i de bzhin nyid deni gcig pa nyid de gnyisasu ma mchis shing gnyisu dbyer ma mchis so/de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu gang
61b6(khri pa): lags pa nyid rnam pa thams can mkhyen pa nyid lags la rnaM pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid gang lags pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobabs bcu la gas so//de bzhin du sbyar te sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco
61b7(khri pa): brgyad kyi bar gyi de bzhin nyid gang lagasapa dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nid kyi de bzhin nyid gang lags/de bzhin nyid ni gcig pa nyid de gnyis su ma mchis shing gnyis su dbyer ma mchis so//de skad
61b8(khri pa): ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis lha de dag la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//lha dag de bzhin no//ji skad smras pa de bzhin te/gzugs nyid rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid yin
62a1(khri pa): @#//la rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid gzugs yin no//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /de bzhin du 'du shes dang /de bzhin du 'du byed rnams dang /de bzhin du rnam par shes 
62a2(khri pa): pa nyid rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid yin la rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid rnam par shes pa yin te/gzugs kyi de bzhin nyid gang yin pa dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i nyid kyi de bzhin nyid gang yinapa'i de 
62a3(khri pa): bzhin nyid de ni gcig pa nyid de gnyis su med cing gnyis su dbyer med do//de bzhin du tshor ba dag/'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa'i de bzhin nyid gang yin ba dang /rnam pa thams can 
62a4(khri pa): mkhyen pa nyid kyi de bzhin nyid gangs yin pa'i de bzhin nyid de ni gcig pa'i de de gnyis su med cing /gnyis su dbyer med do/de bzhin du sbyar te sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar gyi de bzhin 
62a5(khri pa): nyid gang yin pa dang /rna ba thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi de bzhin ni gang yin pa'i de bzhin nyid da ni gcig pa nyid de gnyisu med cing gnyisu dbyer med do//lha dag don gyi dbang 'di yang dag par gzigs 
62a6(khri pa): pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs ni thugs kyi phrin las chung ba la gzhol bar mdzad kyi chos ston pa la ni ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/lha dag kye ma chos 'di ni zab ste/mthong bar dka' ba/rtogs par 
62a7(khri pa): dka' ba/brtag par mi nus pa/zhi ba zhib mo rtags pa'i mkhas pa dang 'dzangs pas rig par bya ba yin te/'di lta ste shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'dir sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi byang 
62a8(khri pa): chub bstan pa 'di ni 'jig rten thams cad dang mi mthun pa yin te/de ni sus kyang mngon par rdzogs par sangs ma rgyas/gang las kyang mngon par rdzogs bar sangs ma rgyas/gang du yang mngon 
62b1(khri pa): par rdzogs par sangs ma rgyas te/lha dag 'di ni gang la gnyis kun tu rgyu ba yang med pa'i chos rnams kyi zab pa nyid yin pa'i phyir ro//lha dag nam mkha' zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos 'di zab bo//lha dag 
62b2(khri pa): de bzhin du de bzhin nyid zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang /chos kyi dbyings zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang/yang dag pa'i mtha' zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dbyings zab pa nyid kyi phir dang /mtha' yas 
62b3(khri pa): shing mthar thug pa med pa zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang 'ong ba med pa dang 'gro ba med pa zab pa nyid kyi phir dag/skye ba med pa dang /'gag pa med pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /rnam par byang ba med 
62b4(khri pa): pa zab pa nyid kyi phyir chosa'adi zab bo//lha dag de bzhin du bdag zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang shes pa po dang /mthong ba po'i bar du zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos 'id zab bo//lha dag de bzhIn du gzuDa
62b5(khri pa): zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos nid zab po//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa zab pa nyid kyi phyir cho 'di zab bo//lha dag debazhin du mig zab 
62b6(khri pa): pa nyid kyi phyir chos 'di zab po//lha dag de bzhin du rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos 'di gzab po//lha dag gzugs rnams zab pa nyid kyi phyir chosi 'di zab po//lha dag
62b7(khri pa): de bzhin du sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg bya dang /chos rnams zab pa nyid kyi phir chos 'di zab bo//lha dag da bzhin du mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i 
62b8(khri pa): khams zab pa nyid kyi phyir gos 'di zab po/lha dag rna ba'i khams dang /sgra'i khams dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams zab pa nyid kyi phyirachos 'di zab po//lha dang de bzhin du sna'i khams dang /

63a2(khri pa): khams zab pa nyid kyi phyir chas 'di zab bo//lha dag lus kyi khams dang /reg bya'i khams dang /lus kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos la 'di zab bo//lha dag yid kyi khams dang /chos 
63a3(khri pa): kyi khams dang /yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i kham zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos ni zab bo//lha dag de bzhIn du sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang /tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /
63a4(khri pa): bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dan bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab gnyid kyi phyir chos 'di zab po//lha dag de 
63a5(khri pa): bzhIn du nang stong pa nyid zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid 
63a6(khri pa): dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dang mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /ngor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang/rang bzhin 
63a7(khri pa): stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang dngos pa med 
63a8(khri pa): pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir chos 'di zab bo//lha dag de bzhin du dran pa nye bar gzhag pa zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang/de bzhin du yang dag par spong ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa dang dbang po dang /
63b1(khri pa): stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan lag dang /'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos 'di zab bo//lha dag de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu zab pa nyid kyi phyir chosa'adi zab bo//lha dag de bzhin
63b2(khri pa): du sbyar te sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad zab pa nyid kyi phyir dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du zab pa nyid kyi phyir chos'di zab bo//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la lha de
63b3(khri pa): dag gis 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ma bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jig rten thams cad dang mi mthun pa'i chos 'di stan to//de ci'i slad du zhe na/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das 'di ni gzugs gzung ba 'am mi gzung bar bgyi
63b4(khri pa): ba'i slad du bstan pa ma lags/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa bzung ba 'am mi gzung bar/bgyi ba'i slad du bstan pa ma lags/de bzhin du mig gzung
63b5(khri pa): ba 'am mi gzung ba'i slad du bstan pa ma la gas/de bzhin du rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid gzung ba 'am mig zung bar bgyi ba'i slad du bstan par ma lags/gzugs rnams
63b6(khri pa): bzung ba 'am mi gzung ba bgyi ba'i slad du bstan pam lags/de bzhin du sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg bya dang /chos rnams gzung ba 'am mi gzung par bgyi ba'i slad du bstan pa ma lags/de bzhin du mig gi

63b8(khri pa): rnam par shes pa'i khams bzung ba 'am mi gzung bara bgyi ba'i slad du bstanapa ma lags/de bzhin dusna'i khams dang /dri'i khams dang /sna'i rnam par shes pa'i khamsgzung ba 'am mi gzung bar

64a3(khri pa): rnam par shes pa'i khams bzung ba 'am mi gzung b.rabagyi ba'i slad du bstan pa lags/de bzhin du sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa gzung ba 'am mi gzung par bgyi ba'i slad du bstanapa ma lags/tshul khrims

64a6(khri pa): pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'du ma byas stong pa nyid dang mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa

64a8(khri pa): pa stong pa nyid dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid gzung ba'am mi gzung bar bgyi ba'i slad du bstan pa ma lags/de bzhin du dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams gzung ba 'am 
64b1(khri pa): mi gzung bar bgyi ba'i slad du bstan pa ma lags/yang dag par spong ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa dang /dbang po dang /stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan lag dang /'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa

64b7(khri pa): bdag gi 'du byed rnams so//bdag rnam par shes pa'o//de bzhin du bdag gi mig go//bdag gi rna ba'o//sna dang lce dang /lus dang /yid kyang de bzhin no//de bzhin du bdag cag gi gzugs

64b9(khri pa): kyang de bzhin no//de bzhan du bdag gi rna ba'i khams dang /sgra'i khams dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /sna'i khams dang /dri'i khams dang /sna'i rnam par shes pa'i khams/lce'i 
65a1(khri pa): @#//khams dang /ro'i khams dang /lce'i rnam par shes pa'i khams dang /lus kyi khams dang /reg bya'i khams dang /lus kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams ngang /yid kyi khams

65a3(khri pa): du bdag tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dag/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang bsam gtan gyi pha rol du phyin pa'o//bdag gi
65a4(khri pa): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//de bzhin du bdagag nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa

65a6(khri pa): rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang bdag
65a7(khri pa): dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid do//bdag gi dngos po med pa'i ngo bo ni stong pa nyid do//de bzhin du bdag cag dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams so//bdag cag gi dran pa nye bar gzhag pa'i rnams so//de bzhin 
65a8(khri pa): du bdag cag yang dag par spong ba rnams dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams dang /dbang po rnams dang /stobs rnams dang /byang chub kyi yan lag rnams so//bdag cag 'phags pa'i lam yan lag

65b4(khri pa): bam po nyi shu gnyis pa/de skad ces gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis lha de dag la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//lha dag de de de bzhin te/lha dag de bzhin no//ji skad smras pa de bzhin 
65b5(khri pa): te/lha dag chos 'di ni gzugs gzung ba 'am mi gzung bar bya ba'i phyir bstan pa ma yin/tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa gzung ba 'am mi gzung par 
65b6(khri pa): bya ba'i phyir bstan pa ma yi na/de bzhin du mig gzung ba 'am mi gzung ba bya ba'i phyir bstan pa ma min/de bzhin du rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid gzung ba 'am mi gzung 
65b7(khri pa): phyir bstan pa ma yin/gzugs rnams gzung ba 'am mi gzung ra bya ba'i phyir bstan pa yin/de bzhin du sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg bya dang /chos rnams gzung ba 'am mig gzung bar bya ba'i phyir/
65b8(khri pa): bstan pa ma yin/de bzhin du mig gi khams dang /gzugs kyi khams dang /mig gi rnam par shes pa'i khams gzung ba 'am mi gzugs bar bya ba'i phyir bstan ma yin/de bzhin du rna ba'i khams 
66a1(khri pa): @#//dang /sgra'i khams dang /rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams gzung ba 'am mi gzung bar bya ba'i phyir bstan pa ma yin/sna'i khams dang /dri'i khams dang /sna'i rnam par 
66a2(khri pa): shes pa'i khams gzung ba'am mi gzung bar bya ba'i phyer bstan pa ma yin/lce'i khams dang /ro'i khams dang /lce'i rnam par shes pa'i khamasagazung ba 'am mi gzung ba 'am mi gzung par bya ba'i phyer bstan pam yin/

66a6(khri pa): yin/de bzhin du nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid

66a8(khri pa): cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dangngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang dngos pa med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid gzung ba 
66b1(khri pa): 'am mi gzung bar bya ba'i phyir bstan pa ma yin/de bzhin du dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams gzung ba 'am mi gzung bar bya ba'i pyir bstan pa ma yin/de bzhIn du yang dag par spong ba rnams dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams

66b3(khri pa): bcu gzung ba 'am mi gzung ra bya ba'i phyir bstan pa yin/de bzhin du sbyar te sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa btso brgyad kyi bar du gzung ba 'am mi gzung bya ba'i phyir bstan pa mayin zhing /lha dag chos 'di
